Chapter 15ISNDchainBlockchain Technology-Based Secure Information Systems and National Defense

Mir Mehedi Rahman, Sayed Abu Sayeed, and Naresh Kshetri

DOI: 10.1201/9781003449515-15

15.1 Introduction

The incorporation of blockchain technology (BT) within the defense sector is of utmost importance in strengthening national security, particularly in an era characterized by technological supremacy [1]. Through the careful integration of BT, the secure transfer of data can be ensured, thereby creating an environment that promotes the development of innovative solutions. The implementation of this approach will contribute to ensuring that the defensive systems exhibit a high level of resilience and effectiveness in effectively mitigating emerging threats. The primary objective of this study is to investigate and resolve concerns pertaining to the reliability and credibility of data within defense collaborations by employing the principles of decentralization, immutability, and transparency [1]. The recent advancements possess the potential to completely transform military tactics, enabling a more streamlined management of the intricacies linked to contemporary warfare and security considerations. The aforementioned statement underscores the importance of continuously adapting and incorporating technological innovations into the framework of the national security apparatus. Through the implementation of the steps, it is anticipated that the security of the defense infrastructure will experience significant enhancements, thereby establishing it as a frontrunner in terms of technical innovation within the broader global defense sector [1].

During this era of rapid digitalization, it is important to prioritize the protection of information systems and the enhancement of national defense measures. It is of utmost importance to address the issue at hand, particularly in light of the increasing global cyber threats and the need to safeguard critical infrastructure [2]. The emergence of BT presents an opportunity to bolster these domains, thereby demonstrating its capacity to fortify the robustness of national security and information systems in the face of sophisticated cyber-attacks. The ISNDchain concept capitalizes on the decentralized, immutable, and transparent characteristics inherent in BT. Its primary objective is to enhance the security of information systems within defense sectors, encompassing a wide range of applications such as submarines, airplanes, radar systems, and other related domains. The present model aims to tackle the urgent requirement for enhanced security measures in response to the escalating cyber threats. The utilization of BT in private and consortium blockchain systems is facilitated by harnessing its distinctive characteristics, such as enhanced security, privacy, immutability, and efficiency [2]. The present study offers an extensive examination of the potential applications of BT and showcases the effectiveness of the ISNDchain framework in mitigating cyber threats. Moreover, our investigation establishes the fundamental basis for prospective investigations concerning the application of BT within the domain of national defense. A comprehensive analysis of the advantages and challenges associated with the implementation of BT in this crucial field is strongly advocated by our research team.

The increasing adoption of BT for enhancing the security of information systems is being observed in diverse industries. This trend is exemplified by the proposed model for student information systems (SISs) in higher education [3]. The aforementioned model highlights the inherent capability of BT to safeguard confidential information by means of a decentralized and unalterable ledger. This, in turn, provides valuable perspectives on the extensive range of potential applications and advantages that extend beyond the realm of academia. Building upon the existing knowledge in the field, our research endeavors to present the ISNDchain model, a pioneering utilization of BT with the primary objective of enhancing the security and reliability of information systems in the domain of national defense. Contrary to the educational emphasis of the mentioned SIS model, ISNDchain has been specifically designed to address the distinct obstacles encountered by national defense mechanisms. Its primary objectives include ensuring the security of communications and operations, as well as safeguarding classified and sensitive military information [3]. Through the utilization of blockchain's inherent attributes, namely, heightened security, privacy, and efficiency, ISNDchain presents a comprehensive framework that aims to establish a robust defense mechanism against the constantly evolving realm of cyber threats [3]. The approach not only signifies a notable transition toward the utilization of cutting-edge technological solutions in the realm of national defense, but it also underscores the versatility of BT in effectively catering to distinct security requirements across various domains. This, in turn, lays the groundwork for prospective investigations and advancements in the field of secure information systems for national defense [3].

The integration of BT represents a significant shift in the management of data integrity and security within the rapidly evolving field of cybersecurity. Blockchain, initially popularized in the realm of cryptocurrency, is now being employed to safeguard critical infrastructures like electronic voting systems and smart cities. The inherent qualities of immutability, decentralization, and consensus processes in BT improve cybersecurity measures [4]. Similarly, advancements in BT significantly enhance the security and privacy of medical records, particularly in the transition from centralized to decentralized systems of health information management [5]. These attributes improve the visibility of the network, effectively mitigate the risks associated with centralized control, and safeguard the integrity of data against unauthorized alterations. BT is essential for safeguarding sensitive data in various industries, including cybersecurity and healthcare. These achievements also demonstrate the potential of BT in enhancing the security and robustness of defense-related data and activities, which are crucial for national security. Critical industries including government, banking, and finance are under attack from new forms of advanced malware such as fileless ransomware and cryptojacking [6]. These attacks are difficult to detect even with advanced security systems and aim at critical components of economic security that are necessary for preserving economic stability [6]. They have the potential to undermine confidence in security systems among the public and government, disrupt operations, and cost a lot of money. The critical nature of these scenarios highlights the immediate requirement for enhanced cybersecurity protocols with BT and advanced incident response plans in all sectors, including the defense.

15.2 Background Study

Aviv et al. [7] identified 16 essential Distributed Information Systems (DISs) Architectural Properties (APs) that offer issues for DIS systems using Grounded Theory. The authors also gave a case study illustrating how distributed ledger technology (DLT) handles DIS APs in a real-world context, giving empirical proof of the DLT's efficacy in servicing DIS APs. DLT and blockchain paradigms are now enabling technologies for the worldwide development of decentralized and trustworthy solutions. The authors demonstrated that DLT might serve as a development platform for a blockchain-native DIS (BN-DIS) that enables the design and development process. The research gap for a holistic DIS development platform revealed in recent research.

Kshetri [8] studied BT's roles in managing transparency, trust, citizen satisfaction, and minimizing corruption so as to improve the efficiency of public service delivery. The author found that BT has the capacity to enable transparency and build citizen's trust in the public service domain while maintaining a sufficient level of privacy. The popular network architecture in almost all areas is client-server architecture, where a central server remains a powerful computer that controls the overall activities or transactions. BT is based on P2P architecture (peer-to-peer distributed nature with no single point of failure) in contrast to a client-server architecture where power doesn't rest on a single computer.

Putro et al. [9] explored the potential of BT to improve the security and dependability of information systems, with a specific emphasis on Indonesian businesses. Through interviews and surveys, it is determined that startups encounter cybersecurity obstacles. However, they perceive blockchain's decentralization, transparency, and immutability as potential remedies. Although there is a strong interest in blockchain, there are worries over its complex nature and execution. The study concludes that blockchain has the potential to improve data security and offers recommendations for startups and policymakers on adopting this technology effectively.

Cerchione et al. [10] examined the creation and execution of a blockchain-powered Internet of Things (IoT) infrastructure specifically designed for the defense sector. The primary objective of this platform is to effectively handle transactions and information in the supply chain by ensuring transparency, security, and traceability. Additionally, it promotes collaboration among the various participants in the network. The research aims to develop a blockchain model that is both private and approved, with a specific focus on overcoming issues related to throughput, latency, interoperability, and data storage costs. Additionally, the research emphasizes the benefits of this model, including improved security, transparency, and efficiency in supply chain operations. This paper provides valuable theoretical insights and practical instructions for incorporating BT into digital industrial ecosystems, with a specific focus on its ability to transform supply chain management in the defense sector and other industries.

Chen et al. [11] discussed a thorough analysis of security concerns in BT, examining vulnerabilities across all layers of the system, from fundamental components to application layers. The proposal presents a systematic framework to tackle these security concerns, employing experiments such as smart contract verification to showcase the efficacy of the suggested remedies. In addition, the analysis explores Alibaba's blockchain services, with a specific emphasis on their security protocols, such as the implementation of IDEMIX to enhance the privacy of transactions. The study seeks to enhance the security of blockchain by employing a multi-layered strategy, providing valuable insights into both the theoretical and practical elements of defense measures for blockchain. The article emphasizes the intricate nature of ensuring the security of blockchain-based services at every level of the technology stack. It provides a comprehensive approach to finding and resolving security problems. This article enhances the ongoing discussion on blockchain security by offering a comprehensive examination of the obstacles and potential remedies from a complete and integrated viewpoint.

15.3 Information Systems

BT is transforming various industries by surpassing traditional limitations and establishing itself as a formidable presence in the growing realm of information technology. The healthcare sector is one of those that is just about ready to embrace it. Currently, information systems employ BT to enhance the security, confidentiality, and interoperability of data, successfully addressing ongoing challenges in healthcare information management [12]. Blockchain-based systems offer a high degree of data security and traceability, ensuring that healthcare information remains unaltered and freely accessible. This promotes trust and confidence among all parties involved. These advancements significantly improve the underlying framework of information systems by integrating measures to prevent unwanted alterations and data loss, hence enhancing the system's reliability and trustworthiness [12]. Furthermore, BT empowers patients with full control over their data, enabling them to effortlessly oversee consent and exchange information across many platforms while upholding confidentiality [12]. The decentralized structure of blockchain mitigates the risks associated with centralized data storage, hence reducing susceptibility to cyber-attacks and data breaches. This enhances the security of the information system by fortifying its defenses against external attacks. Healthcare businesses are currently adopting consortium blockchains, which provide a method of data governance by allowing several groups to share responsibilities and advantages in a fair manner [12]. The collaborative paradigm not only streamlines clinical and administrative processes but also facilitates the advancement of state-of-the-art technologies, such as secure electronic health records (EHRs), pharmaceutical supply chain management, and transparent billing techniques [12]. Through the implementation of these enhancements, BT not only integrates into existing information systems but also significantly fortifies their general framework, operational efficiency, and data management capabilities. The progress of BT is anticipated to greatly boost healthcare by enhancing the quality of healthcare services, optimizing processes, and improving patient satisfaction.

The combination of BT and evolutionary interval fuzzy models offers a new method to enhance security and intelligence in information systems used in the defense sector. BTs decentralized and secure nature offers strong defense against cyber threats, ensuring the integrity of critical national security data, including sensitive information on military operations and assets [13]. Evolutionary interval fuzzy models provide advanced analysis techniques that can successfully manage the uncertainties and complexities related to objects that are widely distributed [13]. This collaboration leads to the creation of an innovative hybrid model that not only protects information systems from cyber threats but also improves decision-making by offering precise and current data analyses [13]. These enhancements have the ability to significantly improve information system strategies by guaranteeing the secrecy and quality of data, as well as aiding information systems strategists in making informed decisions. The aim of this model is to improve the security and efficiency of information systems by utilizing the secure and transparent characteristics of BT, along with the adaptable and accurate analysis provided by fuzzy models. This hybrid method primarily focuses on safeguarding information systems against the latest digital threats and adapting to the intricacies of contemporary cyber challenges, ensuring robust information security [13].

The incorporation of BT into information systems, as proposed in the ISNDchain, aims to achieve significant progress in developing stronger cybersecurity frameworks. The primary benefit of this concept lies in its capacity to leverage the inherent attributes of BT, like decentralization, immutability, and transparency, to safeguard critical military data from cyber threats. Blockchain, in contrast to traditional centralized systems, disperses data throughout a network of nodes, which greatly increases the difficulty for attackers to illicitly modify or steal data. Furthermore, the immutable characteristic of blockchain ensures that once a transaction is documented, it is impossible to alter or delete, thereby establishing an indisputable record of all transactions. Integrating this capability is crucial for maintaining the genuineness of defense-related data, as the accuracy and reliability of information can directly affect national security and defense operations [14]. By incorporating BT, the security capabilities of defense information systems are enhanced, leading to a secure method of maintaining and exchanging information inside the national defense ecosystem. By embracing the ISNDchain concept, nations may safeguard themselves against the evolving nature of cyber threats and construct a future marked by secure, streamlined, and transparent military operations.

Conventional information systems are vulnerable to cyber-attacks because of their centralized structures. These configurations result in weaknesses that can be exploited by assailants [15]. By incorporating BT, these systems can be further fortified due to their decentralized structure, which mitigates the dangers linked to centralization and bolsters resilience against cyber threats. The immutable nature of blockchain guarantees the integrity of data by preventing unauthorized modifications, making it crucial for managing sensitive information like national defense data [15]. The incorporation of advanced cryptographic algorithms within BT enhances security by facilitating the seamless detection of unwanted modifications. Moreover, the BT offers a clear and verifiable record of transactions, enabling enhanced supervision and prompt actions against possible security breaches. This capability is particularly vital in high-risk scenarios. Stringent security measures are needed to safeguard confidential data against hackers, breaches, and malfunctions. This underscores the importance of implementing stringent security protocols and regulations such as the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) in order to safeguard the confidentiality and integrity of data [15]. In addition, the resilience of BT in withstanding common threats such as unauthorized access and data breaches, along with its capacity to uphold strong levels of privacy and confidentiality, is in line with strict data protection rules like the GDPR. Therefore, incorporating BT into conventional information security frameworks not only reduces current threats but also improves the overall security position, particularly in critical sectors such as national defense where data integrity and confidentiality are of utmost importance [15].

Automated information systems (AISs) utilized for processing ecological data have several obstacles, such as data duplication, intricate file management, and interdependencies between programs and data, which undermine both efficiency and security [16]. By using BT, these systems can experience a substantial improvement in both security and efficiency. The decentralized structure of blockchain eliminates the existence of centralized points of failure, hence enhancing the resilience of the system. The immutable ledger guarantees that data, once recorded, cannot be modified without consensus, hence maintaining data integrity, which is crucial for safeguarding sensitive ecological information. Furthermore, the resilient cryptographic capabilities of blockchain offer powerful defense against data breaches, thereby mitigating the weaknesses observed in conventional AIS. Furthermore, through the reduction of data duplication and the optimization of management procedures [16], blockchain enhances operational efficiency. The progress in BT makes it an essential innovation for modernizing AIS, particularly for systems that store crucial ecological management data. It provides a more secure, efficient, and dependable framework for managing data.

15.4 National Defense and Security

The realization of security and defense goals for nation-states is the focus area of cyber power nations. The military conflict between several countries – Russia vs. Ukraine, South Korea vs. North Korea, Israel vs. Palestine, and China vs. India creates another level of worry among the lines of defense, security communities, and political stability. The involvement of the UN, EU, and NATO in the Anti-Terrorist Operation still cannot control diplomatic factors such as food, energy, and nuclear blackmail, etc. [17]. When solving security and defense issues, conditions of hybrid war via the involvement of third nations like the United States or the United Kingdom play a vital role. Solving state economic crises and “enemy” occupied territories are primary military infrastructure under national defense and national security today.

Advanced operations are being used to improve both military and civilian defense against a wide range of threats. War is always changing, so strong logistics systems are needed that can change the changing needs of both military and civilian defense systems. The logistics of defense and security in the setting of hybrid warfare show the need for a combined method to keep military operations going and civilians strong [18]. Furthermore, economic tactics, especially the growth of tourism, can help strengthen national defense. Economic growth and defense plans working together make sure that economic growth directly improves national security. By getting locals involved in the economic boost that tourism brings, the program also hopes to encourage people to feel responsible and take part in national defense efforts, making safety a goal that everyone in the community shares. For example, the Tourism Village Government Program in Indonesia combines economic growth with state defense efforts. This is a good way to make national defense stronger in the long term. This plan not only makes the economy more stable but also makes the whole country safer and more ready for defense [18].

Critical infrastructure includes vital systems like electricity grids, water supply, and telecommunications, which are essential for everyday life and economic stability. There is a huge risk of cyber-attacks on these infrastructures, such as those experienced by Estonia and Iran, showing how damaging they can be to a country's security and functionality. Cybersecurity is now as important as traditional defense methods. Countries need to protect their infrastructure not just physically but also in the cyber realm to prevent and mitigate attacks. Because cyber threats can come from anywhere and do not respect national borders, international cooperation is also crucial. Countries need to work together to improve defenses against these threats. Enhanced security measures, both digital and physical, are essential to keep these systems safe [19].

Another interesting aspect is Neurodivergent individuals, such as those with autism, Attention Deficit / Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD), and dyslexia, already work in various national security roles including intelligence officers and security analysts. Their unique cognitive abilities, like pattern recognition and attention to detail, are highly valuable in addressing complex security challenges. The cognitive diversity provided by neurodivergent individuals enhances problem-solving and analytical capabilities within national security organizations, contributing to more innovative and effective security solutions. However, recruiting and retaining neurodivergent talent is challenging due to biases and workplace practices not designed for them. Many neurodivergent individuals may hide their condition to avoid discrimination, potentially impacting their performance and well-being. Embracing neurodiversity can strengthen national security organizations by expanding the range of cognitive skills available to tackle security threats, making these agencies more adaptive and resilient [20].

The Indonesian government recognizes the strategic significance of its maritime resources and has prioritized maritime security education to bolster national defense. Integrating maritime security education into the national curriculum is seen as essential for fostering a knowledgeable society that can contribute to maritime security and national defense. Indonesia faces numerous threats such as piracy, illegal fishing, and smuggling and education is key in equipping citizens with the knowledge to mitigate these threats. By bolstering the Navy's capabilities and promoting a resilient maritime workforce, Indonesia is proactively enhancing its defensive and strategic capabilities against potential maritime threats [21].

15.5 Blockchain for Security and Defense

Utilizing the core features of BT such as decentralization, immutability, and smart contracts can significantly enhance security in the defense sector [22]. By removing centralized sources of failure, which are especially vulnerable to cyber-attacks in military networks, decentralization reduces risks. Data integrity must be preserved for procedures where altered data could have severe effects, and immutability ensures this. By automating processes and ensuring adherence to standards before transactions are completed, smart contracts have the potential to revolutionize supply chain management and defense logistics, thereby improving the security of equipment handling. Furthermore, the robust cryptographic properties of blockchain facilitate communication and operations management, ensuring dependable, fast, and secure data transmission within the defense industry. Controlled access ensures the necessary secrecy while allowing clear visibility of audit trails which is an essential component of operational security. National defense frameworks are updated and significantly strengthened by these components, which also give them resistance to evolving threats. Blockchain can be carefully improved to become an essential technology for increasing defense capabilities. It provides a structure that encourages rigorous security measures and accountability [22].

The military industry needs BT because it efficiently addresses a number of technological security challenges, including reducing data tampering, enhancing privacy, and reducing cyber dangers like double spending and the 51% attack [23]. For military systems that demand unwavering data secrecy and system stability, the decentralized structure of blockchain improves system resilience by removing centralized vulnerabilities [23]. Furthermore, the unchangeability of BT ensures the accuracy of documented information, which is essential for maintaining precise records of defense-related transactions and activities [23]. Multi-node collaborative defense, one of the advanced security models, uses several nodes to monitor, back up, reach consensus, and dynamically update security policies in response to possible assaults, significantly improving system security [23]. This well-coordinated approach enhances the group's ability to resist complex cyber-attacks while fortifying the protection system against individual node failures [23]. Additionally, BT can improve transparent and safe communication between different defense industry divisions and inside the sector itself, ensuring that all operations are documented and open to review [23]. In military situations, it is critical to maintain a high standard of operational integrity and ensure accountability. In this situation, putting BT to use makes it easier to create a defense structure that is not only strong and efficient but also adaptable to the ever-changing landscape of cybersecurity threats [23].

A method to enhance the security of software-defined networks (SDNs) in industrial IoT systems is proposed in [24]. The approach involves the integration of entropy measurements with BT [24]. The objective of this method is to detect and mitigate Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS) attacks, which present a significant threat to the security and reliability of networks [24]. The hybrid methodology integrates joint entropy, conditional entropy, and mutual information to build tri-entropy. This method surpasses traditional single entropy measurements and enables comprehensive DDoS detection within the network domain [24]. This comprehensive technique enhances the network's ability to withstand attacks by reducing the occurrence of false positives and increasing the accuracy of detection [24]. A smart contract based on BT is used to document the details of any identified assaults, ensuring that they cannot be changed and providing transparency across multiple areas [24]. Blockchain's decentralized structure and smart contract features enable secure cross-domain communication, facilitating rapid and coordinated reactions to attacks [24]. This integration facilitates the rapid neutralization of attack patterns across the network by improving defensive strategies between different domains and reducing attacks inside a single domain [24]. This technique provides a comprehensive answer to complex cybersecurity issues in SDN-based Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) environments [24] by combining BT with traditional network security mechanisms. This technique can be employed in defense to safeguard crucial military networks from cyber threats by detecting and mitigating DDoS attacks, enhancing network resilience, and ensuring the integrity and transparency of communication channels across multiple defense domains.

Different industries are being transformed by BT, which has the ability to revolutionize them all. This is demonstrated by architectures like HNMblock. It can enhance well-being, enhance the security of the medical system, and improve epidemiological surveillance [25]. HNMblock leverages the unchangeable nature and openness of BT to facilitate the immediate and secure monitoring of epidemiological data, allowing for prompt responses to disease outbreaks [25]. Through the utilization of smart contracts and advanced encryption, this solution places patient privacy as a top priority while simultaneously enhancing the security of the medical system [25]. HNMblock facilitates personalized healthcare by promoting active patient involvement and making decisions based on data analysis. BT enhances data security, cyber protection, and data storage integrity in the healthcare industry by eliminating existing impediments in data management systems. Blockchain enhances human wellness, enhances the security of the medical system, and increases transparency in epidemiological surveillance through decentralized ecosystems that encourage data ownership and patient involvement [25]. Blockchain has the capacity to fundamentally transform the healthcare industry, despite challenges such as scalability and regulatory compliance. It offers methods to combat drug counterfeiting, simplify the consolidation of health records, and facilitate predictive modeling. Adopting BT in the healthcare industry requires overcoming obstacles related to organization, technology, and legal regulations. This necessitates the development of scalable frameworks, educational initiatives, and awareness campaigns. In addition to concerns regarding privacy and trust, the transition from traditional models to decentralized paradigms presents challenges in terms of access control and data ownership [25]. However, BT has significant promise in enhancing healthcare outcomes, promoting cooperation, and safeguarding patient information. Moreover, the military industry has the potential to see a significant transformation if it embraces BT, as exemplified by the HNMblock model in the healthcare sector [25]. Defense systems can enhance their cybersecurity protocols and ensure the integrity and confidentiality of crucial military data by leveraging the robust security capabilities and decentralized structure of BT [25]. The immutability of BT can enhance data management and communication inside defense networks by facilitating secure and transparent transactions among many branches and organizations. Furthermore, the incorporation of smart contracts via BT has the potential to improve operational efficiency and responsiveness in critical defense activities by automating security protocols and improving administrative procedures. In summary, the HNMblock model in healthcare offers valuable insights that can be useful for enhancing security, efficiency, and resilience in the defense industry.

BT has the potential to significantly benefit the defense sector by enhancing security, efficiency, and transparency inside the Department of Defense (DoD). It enumerates some notable advantages: the historical and irreversible nature of blockchain enhances auditability, a crucial factor in managing complex contracts and acquisitions. This feature ensures that all transactions can be traced and reduces the risk of fraud and manipulation [26]. Furthermore, the decentralized structure of the system reduces the need for repeated checks, allowing several stakeholders to access a single, verified version of the truth. This expedites procurement operations by minimizing duplicative verifications [26]. This ensures the accuracy and consistency of data while also reducing the amount of time required [26]. Furthermore, BT improves operational security and logistics by facilitating meticulous monitoring of asset transfers and custody changes, which are essential aspects of defense operations [26]. Moreover, blockchain's ability to connect with other systems enables interoperability, reducing operating obstacles between government departments and enhancing data sharing [26]. The report emphasizes that the introduction of blockchain in the DoD must be meticulously prepared to address the sector's specific requirements and constraints, despite its potential to change the industry. Implementing this will guarantee that the technology aligns with existing systems and enhances overall mission efficacy [26].

15.6 Proposed Model – ISNDchain Model

The proposed model, ISNDchain utilizes blockchain structure, ethical defense proposal, EAMV (ethics, authentication, monitoring, verification) model, and SugarChain model [27–30] for cryptographic hash, phase-wise security defense, cybercrime, and agribusiness. The powerful features of BT such as cryptography, immutability, consensus mechanism, trust, transparency, decentralization, non-intermediaries, privacy, programmability, and verifiability paves the way for secure information systems and national defense countermeasures.

The ISNDchain model as proposed (see Figure 15.1) can be described below as it can be used for information system security and national defense level. ISNDchain utilizes several previous models for hash, defense, cybercrime, and agribusiness. A private blockchain can be utilized for national defense because anyone can suggest new blocks in the public blockchain.

  1. Backbone network: The backbone network is the network that interconnects the ISNDchain model with BT included as private blocks for this proposed model.

  2. Threats and challenges: Several threats and challenges revealed for national defense and information systems. Some of them (but not limited to) are security threats (internal and external), military challenges, several external factors, and conflicts between cyber power nations.

  3. Block nodes: Block nodes are connected to backbone networks (embedded with BT) that interlink several modes of ISNDchain from national defense to information systems security.

  4. Connected devices: Like all other information systems, the devices are connected but directly to the backbone network of the ISNDchain model. The connected devices are used to access public defense services as of currently utilizing private blockchain.

  5. Information systems security: The information system security always plays a vital role and is only after governance and budget and is connected to the block 1 of the ISND model.

  6. Governance and budget: Governance as well as budget seems to be an important factor in achieving system security with the utilization of the resources available, connected to block 2 of the ISNDchain model.

  7. Technology and resources: As we are embedding BT with information systems, several other advancement technologies need to be integrated and now connected as block 3 of the ISNDchain model.

  8. National security and defense: The primary objective of national defense as the first line of a national strategy is to combat several external factors (beneath the backbone networks) and connected to the backbone network of our system and connected as block 4 of the ISNDchain model.

15.7 Conclusion

Although BT is used in several areas including finance, agriculture, and business, we can utilize blockchain to handle cyber threats at national defense. Due to the continuous attacks and threats on our critical infrastructures, the ISNDchain model can be a milestone for information systems and national defense. As several blockchain projects have already demonstrated strong security features, they are the primary field of attention in the cyber defense field. Blockchain-based national defense and information systems make use of a decentralized, reliable, highly trusted ledger that stores a series of transactions in the form of linked block sequences. The avoidance of the double spending problem by bitcoin as electronic cash in a trustless environment and generation of blocks via Proof of Work refers to a valid and secure chain network as the foundation of ISNDchain. The cooperation of cryptography with DLT, distributed nodes on distributed networks without any high-level or superior control can serve better for police, military, and defense applications.

15.8 Future Scope

The success of blockchain for bitcoin and Ethereum apart from business, supply chain, and agriculture has already proved the potential and use cases of BT. The future of national defense, cyber defense, and information systems security is largely dependent on advanced technology, their use, and enhancement over several phases. Today security relies on national defense, cyber power, technology used by military or police, and several cyber defense capabilities. The proposed model can be used by governments, states, business firms, and the defense industry to strengthen their cyber activity. Although we have proposed the blockchain-based model primarily for four blocks (national security and defense, technology and resources, governance and budget, and information systems security), it can be expanded to other government entities and subdivisions. Blockchain will serve as a promising technology in achieving several facets other than national defense and information security of governments, connected devices, critical infrastructures, and nation-states.
